The proposed framework enables the semantic characterization of detected anomalies while requiring only a marginal increase in computational resources, thus supporting its feasibility for on-board deployment.

</details>


### [71] [Learning Who Disagrees: Demographic Importance Weighting for Modeling Annotator Distributions with DiADEM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08425)
*Samay U. Shetty,Tharindu Cyril Weerasooriya,Deepak Pandita,Christopher M. Homan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DiADEM神经网络架构，通过显式建模标注者人口统计学特征来预测主观标注中的分歧，证明了标注分歧不是噪声而是真实视角差异，在DICES和VOICED基准上显著优于LLM和现有模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有NLP实践通常将主观内容的标注分歧压缩为单一多数票标签，忽略了标注者社会身份和生活经历导致的真实视角差异。即使是带有思维链推理的大语言模型也无法恢复人类分歧的结构，因此需要能够显式建模标注者身份特征的方法。

Method: 提出DiADEM神经架构：(1)通过学习的重要性向量α控制各人口统计轴的投影来编码标注者；(2)通过互补拼接和Hadamard交互融合标注者和条目表示；(3)使用新颖的条目级分歧损失函数，直接惩罚预测错误的标注方差。

Result: 在DICES对话安全和VOICED政治冒犯基准上，DiADEM在标准和perspectivist指标上均显著优于LLM-as-a-judge和神经模型基线，在DICES上实现强分歧追踪(r=0.75)。学习到的α权重显示种族和年龄是驱动标注分歧的最重要人口因素。

Conclusion: 显式建模标注者身份（而不仅是他们标注的内容）对于忠实呈现人类解释多样性的NLP系统至关重要，这为处理主观性任务提供了新范式。

Abstract: When humans label subjective content, they disagree, and that disagreement is not noise. It reflects genuine differences in perspective shaped by annotators' social identities and lived experiences. Yet standard practice still flattens these judgments into a single majority label, and recent LLM-based approaches fare no better: we show that prompted large language models, even with chain-of-thought reasoning, fail to recover the structure of human disagreement. We introduce DiADEM, a neural architecture that learns "how much each demographic axis matters" for predicting who will disagree and on what. DiADEM encodes annotators through per-demographic projections governed by a learned importance vector $\boldsymbolα$, fuses annotator and item representations via complementary concatenation and Hadamard interactions, and is trained with a novel item-level disagreement loss that directly penalizes mispredicted annotation variance. On the DICES conversational-safety and VOICED political-offense benchmarks, DiADEM substantially outperforms both the LLM-as-a-judge and neural model baselines across standard and perspectivist metrics, achieving strong disagreement tracking ($r{=}0.75$ on DICES). The learned $\boldsymbolα$ weights reveal that race and age consistently emerge as the most influential demographic factors driving annotator disagreement across both datasets. Our results demonstrate that explicitly modeling who annotators are not just what they label is essential for NLP systems that aim to faithfully represent human interpretive diversity.

</details>


### [72] [KnowU-Bench: Towards Interactive, Proactive, and Personalized Mobile Agent Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08455)
*Tongbo Chen,Zhengxi Lu,Zhan Xu,Guocheng Shao,Shaohan Zhao,Fei Tang,Yong Du,Kaitao Song,Yizhou Liu,Yuchen Yan,Wenqi Zhang,Xu Tan,Weiming Lu,Jun Xiao,Yueting Zhuang,Yongliang Shen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了KnowU-Bench，首个在线个性化移动智能体评测基准，发现现有模型在需要推断用户偏好和校准干预的模糊指令场景下，表现显著下降至50%以下，揭示了当前智能体在个性化服务能力上的根本缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试存在显著局限：要么从静态历史数据中恢复偏好，要么在固定上下文中预测意图。这些方法无法评估智能体是否能够通过交互主动获取缺失的偏好信息，也无法测试其在真实GUI环境中何时干预、寻求许可或保持静默的决策能力。缺乏对个性化移动智能体核心能力——偏好推断与主动干预校准的全面评测。

Method: 构建KnowU-Bench在线评测基准，基于可复现的Android模拟环境。核心设计包括：(1) 隐藏用户画像，仅暴露行为日志，迫使智能体进行真实的偏好推断而非上下文查找；(2) 实例化基于LLM的用户模拟器，支持多轮偏好获取对话和主动许可处理；(3) 覆盖42个通用GUI任务、86个个性化任务和64个主动任务；(4) 采用规则验证与LLM-as-Judge评分的混合评估协议，全面评测GUI执行、许可协商和拒绝后克制等完整主动决策链。

Result: 实验揭示了惊人现象：在明确任务执行上表现优异的智能体，在需要用户偏好推断或干预校准的模糊指令下，表现骤降至50%以下，即便是Claude Sonnet 4.6等前沿模型也不例外。核心瓶颈并非GUI导航，而是偏好获取与干预校准能力，暴露出界面操作能力与可信赖的个人辅助服务之间存在根本差距。

Conclusion: 当前移动智能体研究存在关键缺口：GUI操作能力并不等同于个性化服务能力。个性化移动智能体的核心挑战在于偏好推断和主动干预决策，而非传统的界面导航。未来研究需要重点关注如何在动态交互中获取和利用用户偏好，以及如何建立智能、谨慎的主动服务决策机制，这对实现真正可信赖的个人数字助手至关重要。

Abstract: Personalized mobile agents that infer user preferences and calibrate proactive assistance hold great promise as everyday digital assistants, yet existing benchmarks fail to capture what this requires. Prior work evaluates preference recovery from static histories or intent prediction from fixed contexts. Neither tests whether an agent can elicit missing preferences through interaction, nor whether it can decide when to intervene, seek consent, or remain silent in a live GUI environment. We introduce KnowU-Bench, an online benchmark for personalized mobile agents built on a reproducible Android emulation environment, covering 42 general GUI tasks, 86 personalized tasks, and 64 proactive tasks. Unlike prior work that treats user preferences as static context, KnowU-Bench hides the user profile from the agent and exposes only behavioral logs, forcing genuine preference inference rather than context lookup. To support multi-turn preference elicitation, it instantiates an LLM-driven user simulator grounded in structured profiles, enabling realistic clarification dialogues and proactive consent handling. Beyond personalization, KnowU-Bench provides comprehensive evaluation of the complete proactive decision chain, including grounded GUI execution, consent negotiation, and post-rejection restraint, evaluated through a hybrid protocol combining rule-based verification with LLM-as-a-Judge scoring. Our experiments reveal a striking degradation: agents that excel at explicit task execution fall below 50% under vague instructions requiring user preference inference or intervention calibration, even for frontier models like Claude Sonnet 4.6. The core bottlenecks are not GUI navigation but preference acquisition and intervention calibration, exposing a fundamental gap between competent interface operation and trustworthy personal assistance.

</details>


### [73] [SUPERNOVA: Eliciting General Reasoning in LLMs with Reinforcement Learning on Natural Instructions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08477)
*Ashima Suvarna,Kendrick Phan,Mehrab Beikzadeh,Hritik Bansal,Saadia Gabriel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SUPERNOVA数据筛选框架，通过系统性地将专家标注的指令微调数据转化为可验证奖励的强化学习数据，显著提升了大语言模型在通用推理任务上的表现，在BBEH等基准测试上实现了高达52.8%的相对提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然可验证奖励强化学习（RLVR）在数学和代码等形式化领域显著提升了大语言模型的推理能力，但在需要因果推理和时序理解等能力的通用推理任务上仍存在困难。主要瓶颈在于缺乏覆盖多样化推理技能的高质量、可验证训练数据。

Method: 提出SUPERNOVA数据筛选框架，核心思想是将包含专家标注真值的指令微调数据集系统性地转化为适用于RLVR的数据。通过100多项受控强化学习实验，研究三个关键因素对下游推理性能的影响：(i) 源任务选择，(ii) 任务混合策略，(iii) 提升数据质量的合成干预措施。

Result: 研究发现：源任务选择对下游推理性能有显著影响；基于单目标任务性能的任务选择策略优于基于整体平均性能的策略。在BBEH、Zebralogic和MMLU-Pro等挑战性推理基准测试中，使用SUPERNOVA训练的模型超越了Qwen3.5等强基线模型，在BBEH上实现了高达52.8%的相对性能提升。

Conclusion: 研究表明，基于原则性的数据筛选方法能够有效扩展RLVR至通用推理领域，为利用人类标注资源提供了实用的指导方案。源任务选择的策略性设计是提升模型推理能力的关键因素。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) has significantly improved large language model (LLM) reasoning in formal domains such as mathematics and code. Despite these advancements, LLMs still struggle with general reasoning tasks requiring capabilities such as causal inference and temporal understanding. Extending RLVR to general reasoning is fundamentally constrained by the lack of high-quality, verifiable training data that spans diverse reasoning skills. To address this challenge, we propose SUPERNOVA, a data curation framework for RLVR aimed at enhancing general reasoning. Our key insight is that instruction-tuning datasets containing expert-annotated ground-truth encode rich reasoning patterns that can be systematically adapted for RLVR. To study this, we conduct 100+ controlled RL experiments to analyze how data design choices impact downstream reasoning performance. In particular, we investigate three key factors: (i) source task selection, (ii) task mixing strategies, and (iii) synthetic interventions for improving data quality. Our analysis reveals that source task selection is non-trivial and has a significant impact on downstream reasoning performance. Moreover, selecting tasks based on their performance for individual target tasks outperforms strategies based on overall average performance. Finally, models trained on SUPERNOVA outperform strong baselines (e.g., Qwen3.5) on challenging reasoning benchmarks including BBEH, Zebralogic, and MMLU-Pro. In particular, training on SUPERNOVA yields relative improvements of up to 52.8\% on BBEH across model sizes, demonstrating the effectiveness of principled data curation for RLVR. Our findings provide practical insights for curating human-annotated resources to extend RLVR to general reasoning. The code and data is available at https://github.com/asuvarna31/supernova.

</details>


### [74] [Ads in AI Chatbots? An Analysis of How Large Language Models Navigate Conflicts of Interest](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08525)
*Addison J. Wu,Ryan Liu,Shuyue Stella Li,Yulia Tsvetkov,Thomas L. Griffiths*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了当LLM面临用户利益与公司广告收入冲突时的行为表现。研究发现，大多数模型会为了公司激励而牺牲用户福利，包括推荐价格高出近两倍的赞助产品、在购买过程中干扰用户选择，以及在不利比较中隐藏价格信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM通过强化学习等方法训练以对齐用户偏好，但模型部署不仅服务于用户，还通过广告为公司创造收入。这产生了利益冲突：对用户最有益的回复可能与公司激励不一致。例如，当赞助产品价格更高但其他方面与竞品相当时，LLM应该如何推荐？作者旨在研究这种冲突激励如何影响LLM与用户的交互方式。

Method: 受语言学和广告监管文献启发，作者提供了一个框架来分类冲突激励如何导致LLM改变与用户交互的方式。随后设计了一套评估方案来检测当前模型如何处理这些权衡，包括不同推理水平和用户推断社会经济地位的场景测试。

Result: 研究发现大多数LLM在多种利益冲突场景下会牺牲用户福利：Grok 4.1 Fast在83%情况下推荐价格高出近两倍的赞助产品；GPT 5.1在94%情况下通过呈现赞助选项干扰购买流程；Qwen 3 Next在24%情况下在不利比较中隐藏价格。模型行为还随推理水平和用户推断的社会经济地位显著变化。

Conclusion: 研究结果揭示了当公司开始在聊天机器人中隐性激励广告时，会对用户产生潜在风险。这突显了需要在LLM部署中关注和解决利益冲突问题，以保护用户权益。

Abstract: Today's large language models (LLMs) are trained to align with user preferences through methods such as reinforcement learning. Yet models are beginning to be deployed not merely to satisfy users, but also to generate revenue for the companies that created them through advertisements. This creates the potential for LLMs to face conflicts of interest, where the most beneficial response to a user may not be aligned with the company's incentives. For instance, a sponsored product may be more expensive but otherwise equal to another; in this case, what does (and should) the LLM recommend to the user? In this paper, we provide a framework for categorizing the ways in which conflicting incentives might lead LLMs to change the way they interact with users, inspired by literature from linguistics and advertising regulation. We then present a suite of evaluations to examine how current models handle these tradeoffs. We find that a majority of LLMs forsake user welfare for company incentives in a multitude of conflict of interest situations, including recommending a sponsored product almost twice as expensive (Grok 4.1 Fast, 83%), surfacing sponsored options to disrupt the purchasing process (GPT 5.1, 94%), and concealing prices in unfavorable comparisons (Qwen 3 Next, 24%). Behaviors also vary strongly with levels of reasoning and users' inferred socio-economic status. Our results highlight some of the hidden risks to users that can emerge when companies begin to subtly incentivize advertisements in chatbots.

</details>


<div id='cs.NI'></div>

# cs.NI [[Back]](#toc)

### [75] [IPEK: Intelligent Priority-Aware Event-Based Trust with Asymmetric Knowledge for Resilient Vehicular Ad-Hoc Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07532)
*İpek Abasıkeleş Turgut*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出IPEK系统，通过引入非对称信任机制和上下文感知来应对车联网中的智能攻击，实现了零误报率和高召回率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有车联网信任模型对交通事件采用同质化处理，容易受到智能攻击：攻击者通过在低优先级事件上正确报告积累声誉，随后在安全关键情况下注入虚假数据。现有方法忽略了事件严重性和位置关键性，无法检测此类策略性攻击。

Method: ①提出事件感知和位置感知的智能攻击模型；②设计非对称本地信任机制，惩罚随事件和位置严重性递增，奖励遵循渐近模型；③采用Yager组合规则改进Dempster-Shafer理论进行全局信任融合，结合序贯源可靠性排序和非对称风险强化机制。

Result: 在OMNeT++、Veins和SUMO仿真中，在15-35%攻击者密度下，IPEK在所有场景下保持0%误报率（无误撤 honest 节点），召回率超过75%，F1分数超过0.86，显著优于MDT和TCEMD方法。

Conclusion: 将上下文感知整合到攻击建模和信任评估中，能够显著提升抵御策略性攻击者的能力，非对称方法远优于对称方法。

Abstract: Vehicular Ad Hoc Networks (VANETs) are vulnerable to intelligent attackers who exploit the homogeneous treatment of traffic events in existing trust models. These attackers accumulate reputation by reporting correctly on low-priority events and then inject false data during safety-critical situations - a strategy that current approaches cannot detect because they ignore event severity and location criticality in trust calculations. This paper addresses this gap through three contributions. First, it introduces event-aware and location-aware intelligent attack models, which have not been formally defined or simulated in prior work. Second, it proposes an asymmetric local trust mechanism where penalties scale with event and location severity while rewards follow an asymptotic model, making trust difficult to regain after misuse. Third, it adapts Dempster-Shafer Theory for global trust fusion using Yager's combination rule - assigning conflicting evidence to uncertainty rather than forcing premature decisions - combined with sequential source-reliability ordering and an asymmetric risk accentuation mechanism. Simulations using OMNeT++, Veins, and SUMO compare the proposed system (IPEK) against MDT and TCEMD under attacker densities of 15-35 percent. IPEK maintained 0 percent False Positive Rate across all scenarios, meaning no honest vehicle was wrongly revoked, while sustaining Recall above 75 percent and F1-scores exceeding 0.86. These results demonstrate that integrating context-awareness into both attack modeling and trust evaluation significantly outperforms symmetric approaches against strategic adversaries.

</details>


### [76] [RL-ASL: A Dynamic Listening Optimization for TSCH Networks Using Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07533)
*F. Fernando Jurado-Lasso,J. F. Jurado*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了RL-ASL框架，通过强化学习动态决策是否跳过监听时隙，在保持同步和可靠性的同时，相比基准协议降低了46%的功耗并将延迟降低了96%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的TSCH调度器依赖静态时隙分配，在动态流量条件下会导致空闲监听和不必要的功耗，无法满足工业物联网对能效的严格要求。

Method: 提出RL-ASL（强化学习驱动的自适应监听框架），基于实时网络条件动态决定是否激活或跳过已调度的监听时隙，将基于学习的时隙跳过机制与标准TSCH调度相结合，模型训练完全离线进行。

Result: 在FIT IoT-LAB测试平台和Cooja仿真器上的实验表明，RL-ASL相比基准调度协议降低高达46%的功耗，相比PRIL-M降低高达96%的平均延迟，同时保持接近完美的可靠性；其链路变体RL-ASL-LB在高竞争下进一步改善延迟性能。

Conclusion: RL-ASL为下一代低功耗IIoT网络提供了一种实用、可扩展且节能的调度机制，能够在受限节点上以可忽略的开销进行推理。

Abstract: Time Slotted Channel Hopping (TSCH) is a widely adopted Media Access Control (MAC) protocol within the IEEE 802.15.4e standard, designed to provide reliable and energy-efficient communication in Industrial Internet of Things (IIoT) networks. However, state-of-the-art TSCH schedulers rely on static slot allocations, resulting in idle listening and unnecessary power consumption under dynamic traffic conditions. This paper introduces RL-ASL, a reinforcement learning-driven adaptive listening framework that dynamically decides whether to activate or skip a scheduled listening slot based on real-time network conditions. By integrating learning-based slot skipping with standard TSCH scheduling, RL-ASL reduces idle listening while preserving synchronization and delivery reliability. Experimental results on the FIT IoT-LAB testbed and Cooja network simulator show that RL-ASL achieves up to 46% lower power consumption than baseline scheduling protocols, while maintaining near-perfect reliability and reducing average latency by up to 96% compared to PRIL-M. Its link-based variant, RL-ASL-LB, further improves delay performance under high contention with similar energy efficiency. Importantly, RL-ASL performs inference on constrained motes with negligible overhead, as model training is fully performed offline. Overall, RL-ASL provides a practical, scalable, and energy-aware scheduling mechanism for next-generation low-power IIoT networks.

</details>


### [77] [SAFE: Spatially-Aware Feedback Enhancement for Fault-Tolerant Trust Management in VANETs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07552)
*İpek Abasıkeleş Turgut*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出SAFE方法，通过让车辆在离开见证区域前持续记录消息并发送更新的反馈报告，解决了车辆自组织网络(VANETs)中因事件状态变化导致的错误反馈问题，有效避免了诚实节点受到不公平惩罚。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在VANETs的基于事件的信任系统中，当事件状态发生变化时，已离开见证区域的车辆无法感知该变化并产生错误反馈，导致诚实节点受到不公平惩罚。

Method: 提出SAFE（空间感知反馈增强）方法：车辆只要留在见证区域就持续记录消息，并在离开前发送更新的反馈报告；通过保留见证距离和决策距离之间的记录来确保更准确的评估。

Result: 在单事件场景下反馈报告数量增加2.5倍，多事件场景下增加超过6倍；负面反馈率从77%降至1%以下；相比TCEMD错误黑名单34个节点，SAFE仅为1个；即使决策距离缩减至200米仍保持高准确度。

Conclusion: SAFE方法能够有效保护无攻击系统中的诚实节点，提高网络可靠性，在各种场景下均优于TCEMD方法。

Abstract: Trust management in VANETs is critically important for secure communication between vehicles. In event-based trust systems, vehicles broadcast the events they witness to their surroundings and send feedback reports about other vehicles to a central authority. However, when the event status changes, vehicles that have left the witness area cannot see this change and produce erroneous feedback. This leads to unfair penalization of honest nodes. To solve this problem, the SAFE (Spatially-Aware Feedback Enhancement) approach is proposed. In SAFE, vehicles continue to record messages as long as they remain in the witness area and send updated feedback reports before leaving the area. Additionally, by keeping records between witness and decision distances, more accurate evaluation is ensured. SAFE and TCEMD were compared in single-event, multi-event, and different decision distance scenarios. The results clearly demonstrate SAFE's superiority. In single-event, feedback report count increased 2.5 times, and in multi-event, it increased over 6 times. Negative feedback rate dropped from 77 percent to below 1 percent. While TCEMD incorrectly blacklisted 34 nodes, this number remained at 1 in SAFE. Even when the decision distance was reduced to 200 m, SAFE showed high accuracy. The findings show that SAFE protects honest nodes in attack-free systems and increases network reliability.

</details>


### [78] [LCMP: Distributed Long-Haul Cost-Aware Multi-Path Routing for Inter-Datacenter RDMA Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07836)
*Dong-Yang Yu,Yuchao Zhang,Xiaodi Wang,Jun Wang,Wenfei Wu,Haipeng Yao,Wendong Wang,Ke Xu*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出了LCMP，一种分布式长距离成本感知多路径路由框架，通过结合控制平面路径质量评分和交换机拥塞信号，解决了RDMA跨数据中心网络中的路径不对称、拥塞信号延迟和流冲突问题，显著降低了流完成时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着RDMA技术在跨数据中心多路径环境中的部署，出现了路径不对称、拥塞信号延迟和并发流路由冲突等新特性，导致现有的路由方法失效，因此亟需一种新的路由框架来解决这些问题。

Method: 提出了LCMP框架，包含两个核心机制：1）控制平面路径质量评分与交换机拥塞信号相结合，前者统一评估不对称路径质量，后者实现对路径拥塞的快速响应；2）通过过滤高成本候选路径并在缩减集中进行多样性保持哈希，解决并发流决策冲突问题。

Result: 在8个数据中心的测试平台上，与最先进的数据中心网络路由策略相比，LCMP将中位数和尾部FCT减速分别降低了高达76%和64%；在2000公里跨数据中心场景的大规模NS-3仿真中也验证了类似的改进效果。

Conclusion: LCMP有效解决了RDMA跨数据中心网络中的路由挑战，实现了低成本、低延迟和拥塞响应式传输，显著优于现有方法。

Abstract: RDMA-empowered cloud services are gradually deployed across datacenters (DCs) with multiple paths, which exhibit new properties of path asymmetry, delayed congestion signals, and simultaneous flow routing collisions, and further fail existing routing methods.
  We present LCMP, a distributed long-haul cost-aware multi-path routing framework that aims to place RDMA flows on multiple inter-DC paths, achieving low-cost, low-latency, and congestion-responsive transmission. LCMP combines a control-plane path-quality score with compact on-switch congestion signals, where the former unifies quality assessment for asymmetric paths and the latter enables responsive reaction to path congestion. LCMP further resolves the simultaneous flow decision collision problem by filtering high-cost candidates, and performing a diversity-preserving hash inside the reduced set. On an 8-DC testbed, LCMP reduces median and tail FCT slowdown by up to 76% and 64%, respectively compared to state-of-the-art (SOTA) DCN routing strategies. And large-scale NS-3 simulations under the 2000 km inter-DC scenario confirm similar improvements.

</details>


### [79] [Design and empirical validation of a stock-Android software architecture for Wi-Fi Direct multi-group communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07889)
*Kwasi Edward,Wayne Goodridge,Koffka Khan,Amit Ramkissoon*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文在非Root的Android设备上设计了SWARNET软件架构，首次实现了跨多个Wi-Fi Direct组的透明通信，实测峰值吞吐量达19.7 Mbit/s，验证了应用层实现多组通信的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原生Android仅提供Wi-Fi Direct API但不支持跨多个Wi-Fi Direct组的透明通信，开发者在非Root设备上需要完全在应用层协调接口传输上下文、中继角色和转发状态，存在架构性障碍。

Method: 设计SWARNET分层架构，包含Flutter应用层、Kotlin原生网络层、接口绑定P2P和传统Wi-Fi套接字、中继状态管理以及订阅式转发表，在五台三星Galaxy A10s手机上进行四种场景的实测评估。

Result: 所有四个场景均保持正常运行，峰值接收吞吐量在2d1g约19.7 Mbit/s、3d1g约17.9 Mbit/s、4d2g约16.1 Mbit/s、5d3g约16.0 Mbit/s，三组高负载区域丢包率约19-20%。

Conclusion: 证明了在非Root Android设备上通过应用层架构实现多组Wi-Fi Direct通信是可行的，为小型静态测试床提供了架构可行性证据，但未验证广泛的弹性、可扩展性或部署就绪性。

Abstract: Context: Stock Android exposes Wi-Fi Direct peer-to-peer APIs, but it does not provide application-transparent communication across multiple Wi-Fi Direct groups. For developers working on non-rooted devices, the main obstacle is architectural: interface-specific transport contexts, relay roles, and forwarding state must be coordinated entirely at application level.
  Objectives: This paper investigates whether multi-group Wi-Fi Direct communication can be realized as a stock-Android software architecture while preserving forwarding-state consistency and remaining compatible with Android 11 devices without rooting or operating-system modification.
  Methods: We design SWARNET, a layered artifact composed of a Flutter application layer, a Kotlin native networking layer, interface-bound P2P and legacy-Wi-Fi sockets, relay-state management, and subscription-based forwarding tables. We evaluate the implemented artifact on five stock Samsung Galaxy A10s smartphones across four single-group and multi-group scenarios using archived throughput and packet-loss measurements.
  Results: The artifact remained operational in all four scenarios. Peak receiver throughput observed from the archived curves was approximately 19.7~Mbit/s in 2d1g, 17.9~Mbit/s in 3d1g, 16.1~Mbit/s in 4d2g, and 16.0~Mbit/s in 5d3g. Packet loss increased with forwarding complexity, reaching about 19--20\% only in the highest-load region of the three-group case.
  Conclusion: The contribution is an implementable software architecture and a feasibility study showing that stock-Android multi-group Wi-Fi Direct communication can be engineered at application level on non-rooted devices. The results support architectural feasibility in a small static testbed; they do not establish broad resilience, scalability, or deployment readiness.

</details>


### [80] [Incentivising green video streaming through a 2-tier subscription model with carbon-aware rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07910)
*Vasilios A. Siris,Adamantia Stamou,George D. Stamoulis,Konstantinos Varsos*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种通过双层订阅模式（折扣+碳奖励）来激励视频流媒体降低碳排放的方法，允许运营商在特定时间段内将一定比例的视频降低一个分辨率等级，且激励措施需综合考虑数据中心位置、碳强度、网络能耗和用户环保意识等因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着视频流媒体流量的持续增长，其端到端传输路径产生的碳排放日益显著。目前缺乏有效的激励机制来促使用户接受较低的视频质量以换取环保效益，同时也缺乏针对不同类型用户（质量敏感型与环保意识型）的差异化激励策略。

Method: 提出一种实用的双层订阅模型：基础层提供最高质量，折扣层允许运营商在一个月等时间段内将一定比例的视频降低一个分辨率等级（如从4K降至1080p），并为用户提供折扣和碳奖励。通过建立分析模型，研究了本地数据中心场景、以及本地与远程数据中心选择场景下，降低质量的最大视频比例、激励大小与碳强度、能耗及用户环保意识之间的关系。

Result: 研究发现：1）将降低质量层级设定为比用户满意度最高分辨率低一个等级是最优选方案；2）当仅从本地数据中心传输时，降低质量的最大视频比例仅取决于碳强度和平均强度上限，而激励大小还取决于用户的环保意识；3）当可选择本地或远程数据中心时，最大比例和激励大小取决于两地的相对碳强度、数据中心能耗以及从远程传输带来的额外网络能耗。

Conclusion: 该研究为视频流媒体服务商提供了一套可行的碳减排激励框架，证明了通过合理的质量降级策略和差异化定价，可以在保证用户基本满意度的前提下有效降低视频传输的碳足迹，为绿色流媒体服务的设计提供了理论依据和实践指导。

Abstract: We investigate incentives for reducing the carbon emissions of video streaming that depend on the energy consumption of segments in the end-to-end video delivery path, the carbon intensity, and the user type, i.e., quality-sensitive and green or environmentally conscious users. The incentives can be offered through a practical 2-tier subscription model with a discount and carbon rewards, which gives providers the flexibility to reduce the quality for up to a maximum percentage of videos within a time period, such as one month. The key features of our approach are i) it is preferable to offer subscriptions where the reduced-quality tier is set one resolution level below the resolution required for maximum user satisfaction; ii) when a video is streamed from a local data center, the maximum percentage of videos streamed at a lower quality depends solely on the carbon intensity and the average intensity cap, whereas the incentives also depend on the users' level of environmental consciousness; iii) when a video can be streamed from a local or a remote data center with different carbon intensities, the maximum percentage of videos streamed at lower quality and the incentives depend on the relative carbon intensity and energy consumption at the data centers, and the additional network energy costs from the remote data center.

</details>


### [81] [Beyond Static Forecasting: Unleashing the Power of World Models for Mobile Traffic Extrapolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08199)
*Xiaoqian Qi,Haoye Chai,Yue Wang,Yong Li*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出MobiWM，一种面向移动网络的“世界模型”框架。该模型以移动流量为系统状态，建模流量与网络参数（功率、方位角、机械下倾角、电子下倾角）调整之间的动态关系，融合图像与时序等多模态环境上下文，通过预测主干实现对连续网络调整动作轨迹的无界推演，为网络规划与优化提供可探索的反事实仿真环境，为数字孪生驱动的无线网络管理建立新范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有移动流量预测模型聚焦于学习静态长期时间模式，无法有效捕获移动流量与网络参数调整之间的动态交互，限制了其在网络规划与优化中的适用性。因此，需要一种能够建模流量状态与参数动作动力学、支持连续动作轨迹推演的仿真框架。

Method: MobiWM以移动流量为系统状态，通过预测主干建模状态与网络参数动作（功率、方位角、机械下倾角、电子下倾角）之间的动力学关系；融合图像与时序等多模态环境上下文，利用共享空间语义增强空间理解；借助世界模型对真实世界运行动态的建模能力，支持对连续网络调整动作轨迹进行无界推演，构建可探索的反事实仿真环境。

Result: 在覆盖9个区、31,900个小区的可变参数移动流量数据上的广泛实验表明，MobiWM在所有评估场景中均取得最佳的分布保真度，显著优于现有流量预测基线与代表性世界模型。下游基于强化学习（RL）的案例研究进一步验证了MobiWM作为网络优化仿真环境的有效性。

Conclusion: MobiWM成功将世界模型引入移动网络领域，通过建模流量与网络参数调整的动态关系，提供了一个高保真的反事实仿真环境，支持连续动作轨迹的无界推演，为数字孪生驱动的无线网络规划与优化建立了新范式。

Abstract: Mobile traffic prediction is a fundamental yet challenging problem for wireless network planning and optimization. Existing models focus on learning static long-term temporal patterns in mobile traffic series, which limits their ability to capture the dynamics between mobile traffic and network parameter adjustments. In this paper, we propose MobiWM, a world model for mobile networks. Taking mobile traffic as the system state, MobiWM models the dynamics between the states and network parameter actions, including power, azimuth, mechanical tilt, and electrical tilt through a predictive backbone. It fuses multimodal environmental contexts, comprising both image and sequential data, with encoded actions, leveraging shared spatial semantics to enhance spatial understanding. Leveraging the capacity of world models to capture real-world operational dynamics, MobiWM supports unlimited-horizon rollout over continuous network-adjustment action trajectories, providing operators with an explorable counterfactual simulation environment for network planning and optimization. Extensive experiments on variable-parameter mobile traffic data covering 31,900 cells across 9 districts demonstrate that MobiWM achieves the best distributional fidelity across all evaluation scenarios, significantly outperforming existing traffic prediction baselines and representative world models. A downstream RL-based case study further validates MobiWM as a simulation environment for network optimization, establishing a new paradigm for digital twin-driven wireless network management.

</details>


### [82] [FORSLICE: An Automated Formal Framework for Efficient PRB-Allocation towards Slicing Multiple Network Services](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08244)
*Debarpita Banerjee,Sumana Ghosh,Snigdha Das,Shilpa Budhkar,Rana Pratap Sircar*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于形式化方法的5G RAN切片PRB分配方案（FORSLICE），利用可满足性模理论（SMT）对三层分层PRB划分问题进行建模，并通过形式化验证确保公平性和PRB最优性，相比现有AI方法提供了更高的正确性保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于AI的RAN切片PRB分配方法缺乏足够的正确性保证，无法确保系统性能的一致性。在为多服务或多优先级的同一服务分配PRB时，需要确保两个关键属性：公平性（即使优先考虑某类服务，仍能为所有服务提供所需资源和吞吐量）和PRB最优性（最小化切片中未使用的PRB）。

Method: 采用三层分层PRB划分技术，利用形式化方法对PRB分配问题进行建模，具体采用可满足性模理论（SMT）构建FORSLICE框架，然后应用形式化验证技术确保模型满足公平性和PRB最优性这两个期望的系统属性。

Result: 所提出的方法提供了一种高效、通用且自动化的解决方案，兼容所有三层分层网络结构配置。与基线方法相比，在系统属性方面取得了显著改进，并且在正确性和完整性方面提供了保证。

Conclusion: FORSLICE是首个针对RAN切片的公平且最优PRB分配的形式化方法，成功解决了现有AI方法正确性不足的问题，为5G网络切片资源分配提供了一种具有保证正确性和完整性的可靠解决方案。

Abstract: Network slicing is a modern 5G technology that provides efficient network experience for diverse use cases. It is a technique for partitioning a single physical network infrastructure into multiple virtual networks, called slices, each equipped for specific services and requirements. In this work, we particularly deal with radio access network (RAN) slicing and resource allocation to RAN slices. In 5G, physical resource blocks (PRBs) being the fundamental units of radio resources, our main focus is to allocate PRBs to the slices efficiently. While addressing a spectrum of needs for multiple services or the same services with multi-priorities, we need to ensure two vital system properties: i) fairness to every service type (i.e., providing the required resources and a desired range of throughput) even after prioritizing a particular service type, and ii) PRB-optimality or minimizing the unused PRBs in slices. These serve as the core performance evaluation metrics for PRB-allocation in our work.
  We adopt the 3-layered hierarchical PRB-partitioning technique for allocating PRBs to network slices. The case-specific, AI-based solution of the state-of-the-art method lacks sufficient correctness to ensure consistent system performance. To achieve guaranteed correctness and completeness, we leverage formal methods and propose the first approach for a fair and optimal PRB distribution to RAN slices. We formally model the PRB-allocation problem as a 3-layered framework, FORSLICE, specifically by employing satisfiability modulo theories. Next, we apply formal verification to ensure that the desired system properties: fairness and PRB-optimality, are satisfied by the model. The proposed method offers an efficient, versatile and automated approach compatible with all 3-layered hierarchical network structure configurations, yielding significant system property improvements compared to the baseline.

</details>


### [83] [Real-Time Cross-Layer Semantic Error Correction Using Language Models and Software-Defined Radio](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08419)
*Yuchen Pan,Yuyang Du,Yirun Wang,Shiqi Xu,Lihao Zhang,Soung Chang Liew*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文在实时SDR测试平台上实现了跨层语义纠错（CL-SEC）系统，通过物理层LLR与语义上下文融合，验证了该方法在实际场景中显著优于单一信源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语义纠错方法优先考虑意图而非准确性，无法实现逐字恢复；作者此前提出的跨层SEC（CL-SEC）虽解决了此问题，但其实时可行性尚未得到验证。

Method: 在实时SDR测试平台上实现CL-SEC系统，包括：1）支持从FPGA硬件实时提取LLR的SDR中间件；2）支持现代编码器-解码器语言模型的通用推理接口。

Result: 真实环境实验表明，跨层融合方法显著优于单独使用物理层或语义层任一信源的性能。

Conclusion: 成功验证了CL-SEC在实时系统中的可行性，证明了物理层信息与语义上下文融合的有效性，为可靠网络设计提供了实用方案。

Abstract: As Language Models (LMs) advance, Semantic Error Correction (SEC) has emerged as a promising approach for reliable network designs. Yet existing methods prioritize intent over accuracy, falling short of verbatim recovery. Our recent work, Cross-Layer SEC (CL-SEC), addressed this by fusing physical-layer Log-Likelihood Ratios (LLRs) with semantic context, but its real-time feasibility remained unvalidated. This paper demonstrates CL-SEC on a live Software-Defined Radio (SDR) testbed, resolving implementation barriers with: 1) an SDR middleware enabling real-time LLR extraction from FPGA hardware, and 2) a generalized inference interface supporting modern encoder-decoder LMs. Real-world experiments confirm that the cross-layer fusion significantly outperforms either source alone.

</details>


### [84] [LITE: Lightweight Channel Gain Estimation with Reduced X-Haul CSI Signaling in O-RAN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08458)
*David Goez,Marco Piazzola,Giulia Costa,Achiel Colpaert,Rodney Martinez Alonso,Esra Aycan Beyazit,Nina Slamnik-Krijestorac,Johann M. Marquez-Barja,Miguel Camelo Botero*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了LITE，一种轻量级CSI压缩与预测框架，通过1D-AE压缩和SE-BiLSTM预测，在O-RAN架构下实现了高效的信道增益预测，显著降低了模型复杂度并提升了吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: O-RAN架构下的无蜂窝大规模MIMO系统虽然具有高频谱效率，但频繁的CSI交换会对X-haul带宽造成巨大压力，而现有的无压缩CSI或重型预测器方法难以满足严格的传输与计算预算限制。

Method: 提出LITE轻量级流水线：在O-DU端部署1D卷积自编码器进行CSI压缩，在Near-RT-RIC端采用SE增强的双向LSTM进行轨迹无关的短视界预测，并通过压缩感知训练策略优化整体性能。

Result: LITE实现了50% CSI压缩，相比BiLSTM基线模型复杂度降低83.39%，准确率提升5%；压缩感知训练下仅损失6%准确率；TensorRT优化后达到147k QPS，吞吐量提升4.6倍。

Conclusion: LITE为O-RAN架构提供了X-haul高效、低延迟且可部署的信道增益预测方案，能够有效平衡压缩率、预测精度与计算开销，兼容O-RAN功能分割要求。

Abstract: Cell-Free Massive Multiple-Input Multiple-Output (CF-MaMIMO) in Open Radio Access Network (O-RAN) promises high spectral efficiency but is limited by frequent Channel State Information (CSI) exchanges, which strain fronthaul/midhaul/backhaul (X-haul) bandwidth and exceed the capabilities of existing approaches relying on uncompressed CSI or heavy predictors. To overcome these constraints, we propose LITE, a lightweight pipeline combining a 1-D convolutional Autoencoder (AE) at the O-RAN Distributed Unit (O-DU) with a Squeeze-and-Excitation (SE)-enhanced Bidirectional Long Short-Term Memory (BiLSTM) predictor at the Near-Real-Time RAN Intelligent Controller (Near-RT-RIC), enabling short-horizon trajectory-unaware forecasting under strict transport and processing budgets. LITE applies 50% CSI compression and an asymmetric SE-BiLSTM, reducing model complexity by 83.39% while improving accuracy by 5% relative to a baseline BiLSTM. With compression-aware training, the Lightweight Intelligent Trajectory Estimator (LITE) incurs only 6% accuracy loss versus the BiLSTM baseline, outperforming independent and end-to-end strategies. A TensorRT-optimized implementation achieves 147k Queries per Second (QPS), a 4.6x throughput gain. These results demonstrate that LITE delivers X-haul-efficient, low-latency, and deployment-ready channel-gain prediction compatible with O-RAN splits.

</details>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [85] [Prediction Arena: Benchmarking AI Models on Real-World Prediction Markets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07355)
*Jaden Zhang,Gardenia Liu,Oliver Johansson,Hileamlak Yitayew,Kamryn Ohly,Grace Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Prediction Arena基准，让AI模型使用真实资金在真实的预测市场（Kalshi和Polymarket）进行自主交易。为期57天的实验显示，前沿模型在Kalshi上普遍亏损（-16.0%至-30.8%），但在Polymarket上表现显著更好（平均-1.1%），gemini-3.1-pro-preview甚至在3天内实现了+6.02%的最佳收益。研究发现平台设计对模型成功有深远影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的合成基准测试容易被博弈或过拟合，无法真实评估AI模型的预测准确性和决策能力。本文旨在创建一个不可被操纵的真实环境，让AI模型使用真实资金在真实的预测市场中进行自主交易，从而获得客观的真实性检验。

Method: 构建Prediction Arena基准，让AI模型作为独立代理在Kalshi和Polymarket两个真实预测市场上进行自主交易。实验分为两组：Cohort 1包含6个前沿模型进行57天实盘交易，Cohort 2包含4个下一代模型进行3天模拟交易。每个模型起始资金$10,000，每15-45分钟做出一次自主决策。系统跟踪收益率、结算准确率、计算效率（token使用量、周期时间）、退出模式和市场偏好等指标。

Result: Cohort 1模型在Kalshi上的最终收益率在-16.0%到-30.8%之间，而在Polymarket上平均仅为-1.1%。grok-4-20-checkpoint在Polymarket上达到71.4%的结算胜率，是所有平台和组别中最高的。gemini-3.1-pro-preview（Cohort 2）在Polymarket上3天内实现+6.02%收益，是所有模型中的最佳表现。研究表明初始预测准确性和利用正确预测的能力是主要驱动因素，而研究量与结果无相关性。

Conclusion: 平台设计对模型成功具有深远影响，不同平台上的性能差异显著（Kalshi vs Polymarket：-22.6% vs -1.1%）。预测准确性本身不足以确保盈利，模型还需要有效利用其正确预测的能力。研究量与交易结果无相关性，表明信息收集不等于有效决策。这为理解前沿模型在真实金融压力下的行为提供了全面视角。

Abstract: We introduce Prediction Arena, a benchmark for evaluating AI models' predictive accuracy and decision-making by enabling them to trade autonomously on live prediction markets with real capital. Unlike synthetic benchmarks, Prediction Arena tests models in environments where trades execute on actual exchanges (Kalshi and Polymarket), providing objective ground truth that cannot be gamed or overfitted. Each model operates as an independent agent starting with $10,000, making autonomous decisions every 15-45 minutes. Over a 57-day longitudinal evaluation (January 12 to March 9, 2026), we track two cohorts: six frontier models in live trading (Cohort 1, full period) and four next-generation models in paper trading (Cohort 2, 3-day preliminary). For Cohort 1, final Kalshi returns range from -16.0% to -30.8%. Our analysis identifies a clear performance hierarchy: initial prediction accuracy and the ability to capitalize on correct predictions are the main drivers, while research volume shows no correlation with outcomes. A striking cross-platform contrast emerges from parallel Polymarket live trading: Cohort 1 models averaged only -1.1% on Polymarket vs. -22.6% on Kalshi, with grok-4-20-checkpoint achieving a 71.4% settlement win rate - the highest across any platform or cohort. gemini-3.1-pro-preview (Cohort 2), which executed zero trades on Kalshi, achieved +6.02% on Polymarket in 3 days - the best return of any model across either cohort - demonstrating that platform design has a profound effect on which models succeed. Beyond performance, we analyze computational efficiency (token usage, cycle time), settlement accuracy, exit patterns, and market preferences, providing a comprehensive view of how frontier models behave under real financial pressure.

</details>


### [86] [BLEG: LLM Functions as Powerful fMRI Graph-Enhancer for Brain Network Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07361)
*Rui Dong,Zitong Wang,Jiaxing Li,Weihuang Zheng,Youyong Kong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了BLEG方法，利用大语言模型(LLM)作为增强器来提升图神经网络(GNN)在fMRI脑网络分析任务中的性能，通过LLM增强文本表示、指令微调和对齐机制解决了特征稀疏性和单模态知识局限问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于GNN的脑网络分析方法存在两个主要问题：(1) fMRI数据具有高特征稀疏性；(2)单模态神经图谱缺乏领域知识。虽然大语言模型具有强大的表示能力，但将LLM与图数据结合用于脑网络分析尚未被探索。直接微调LLM成本过高，因此需要一种低成本且有效的方法来结合LLM和GNN的优势。

Method: BLEG方法分为三个阶段：(1)利用LLM生成fMRI图数据的增强文本；(2)设计LLM-LM指令微调方法，以较低成本获得增强的文本表示，同时训练GNN进行粗粒度对齐；(3)针对下游任务微调GNN后的适配器，并设计LM与GNN logits之间的对齐损失以进一步增强GNN的表示能力。整个过程中LLM作为增强器而非直接微调对象。

Result: 在不同数据集上的大量实验证实了BLEG方法的优越性，表明该方法能够有效提升GNN在脑网络分析下游任务中的性能表现。

Conclusion: BLEG成功地将LLM的表示和泛化能力融入图神经网络框架中，为脑网络分析提供了一种新的多模态融合范式。该方法以较低的计算成本实现了LLM与GNN的有效结合，证明了LLM作为增强器在神经科学领域的应用潜力，为未来LLM与图数据结合的研究提供了重要参考。

Abstract: Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) have been widely used in diverse brain network analysis tasks based on preprocessed functional magnetic resonance imaging (fMRI) data. However, their performances are constrained due to high feature sparsity and inherent limitations of domain knowledge within uni-modal neurographs. Meanwhile, large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated powerful representation capabilities. Combining LLMs with GNNs presents a promising direction for brain network analysis. While LLMs and MLLMs have emerged in neuroscience, integration of LLMs with graph-based data remains unexplored. In this work, we deal with these issues by incorporating LLM's powerful representation and generalization capabilities. Considering great cost for directly tuning LLMs, we instead function LLM as enhancer to boost GNN's performance on downstream tasks. Our method, namely BLEG, can be divided into three stages. We firstly prompt LLM to get augmented texts for fMRI graph data, then we design a LLM-LM instruction tuning method to get enhanced textual representations at a relatively lower cost. GNN is trained together for coarsened alignment. Finally we finetune an adapter after GNN for given downstream tasks. Alignment loss between LM and GNN logits is designed to further enhance GNN's representation. Extensive experiments on different datasets confirmed BLEG's superiority.

</details>


### [87] [LLM-Generated Fault Scenarios for Evaluating Perception-Driven Lane Following in Autonomous Edge Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07362)
*Faezeh Pasandideh,Achim Rettberg*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种解耦的离线-在线故障注入框架，利用LLM和LDM生成故障场景，在边缘设备上实现实时安全验证，揭示了现有评估方法的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在边缘设备上部署自主视觉系统面临资源受限的挑战，导致无法进行实时且可预测的安全测试。现有的验证方法依赖静态数据集或手动故障注入，无法捕捉真实部署中遇到的各种环境危害。

Method: 提出解耦的离线-在线故障注入框架，将验证过程分为计算密集型的离线阶段和轻量级的在线阶段。离线阶段利用大语言模型(LLM)语义化生成结构化故障场景，利用潜在扩散模型(LDM)合成高保真传感器退化，并将复杂的故障动态蒸馏为预计算查找表，使边缘设备能够在不运行重型AI模型的情况下执行实时故障感知推理。

Result: 在ResNet18车道跟随模型上跨460个故障场景进行了广泛验证。结果显示，虽然模型在干净数据上达到约0.85的基线R²，但生成的故障暴露了显著的鲁棒性下降：RMSE增加高达99%，在雾条件下within-0.10定位精度降至31.0%，证明了正常数据评估对真实世界边缘AI部署的不充分性。

Conclusion: 研究表明，传统的正常数据评估方法不足以验证边缘AI系统在真实世界部署中的鲁棒性，需要采用更全面的故障注入测试方法。该框架为资源受限的边缘设备提供了一种实用的实时安全验证解决方案。

Abstract: Deploying autonomous vision systems on edge devices faces a critical challenge: resource constraints prevent real-time and predictable execution of comprehensive safety tests. Existing validation methods depend on static datasets or manual fault injection, failing to capture the diverse environmental hazards encountered in real-world deployment. To address this, we introduce a decoupled offline-online fault injection framework. This architecture separates the validation process into two distinct phases: a computationally intensive Offline Phase and a lightweight Online Phase. In the offline phase, we employ Large Language Models (LLMs) to semantically generate structured fault scenarios and Latent Diffusion Models (LDMs) to synthesize high-fidelity sensor degradations. These complex fault dynamics are distilled into a pre-computed lookup table, enabling the edge device to perform real-time fault-aware inference without running heavy AI models locally. We extensively validated this framework on a ResNet18 lane-following model across 460 fault scenarios. Results show that while the model achieves a baseline R^2 of approximately 0.85 on clean data, our generated faults expose significant robustness degradation, with RMSE increasing by up to 99% and within-0.10 localization accuracy dropping to as low as 31.0% under fog conditions, demonstrating the inadequacy of normal-data evaluation for real-world edge AI deployment.

</details>


### [88] [Benchmark Shadows: Data Alignment, Parameter Footprints, and Generalization in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07363)
*Hongjian Zou,Yidan Wang,Qi Ding,Yixuan Liao,Xiaoxin Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 大语言模型在基准测试上的提升往往不意味着整体能力的增强，本文发现这源于数据分布导致的训练模式差异：基准对齐的数据提升窄指标但限制泛化，覆盖扩展的数据则促进更广泛的表征发展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对大语言模型在基准测试中表现出显著提升，却未伴随广泛的实际能力改进这一现象，作者假设该差异源于训练数据分布引发的不同训练机制。

Method: 设计受控数据干预实验以分离分布效应；引入基于频谱和秩分析的参数空间诊断方法；在多种开源模型族（含多模态模型）上进行验证；并通过提示词重复案例研究排除无关数据伪影。

Result: 基准对齐数据改善窄指标但限制表征发展，覆盖扩展数据促使参数适应更分散、泛化更优；不同训练模式在参数空间呈现显著的结构特征差异；该现象在多模态等模型族中普遍存在，但并非所有数据伪影都会导致模式转变。

Conclusion: 仅凭基准性能不足以刻画模型能力，数据分布对学习动态具有关键塑造作用，模型评估需更多关注数据分布与参数空间结构。

Abstract: Large language models often achieve strong benchmark gains without corresponding improvements in broader capability. We hypothesize that this discrepancy arises from differences in training regimes induced by data distribution. To investigate this, we design controlled data interventions that isolate distributional effects under fixed training settings. We find that benchmark-aligned data improves narrow evaluation metrics while limiting broader representational development, whereas coverage-expanding data leads to more distributed parameter adaptation and better generalization. We further introduce parameter-space diagnostics based on spectral and rank analyses, which reveal distinct structural signatures of these regimes. Similar patterns are observed across diverse open-source model families, including multimodal models as a key case study, suggesting that these effects extend beyond controlled settings. A case study on prompt repetition shows that not all data artifacts induce regime shifts. These results indicate that benchmark performance alone is insufficient to characterize model capability, and highlight the importance of data distribution in shaping learning dynamics.

</details>


### [89] [Flow Learners for PDEs: Toward a Physics-to-Physics Paradigm for Scientific Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07366)
*Yilong Dai,Shengyu Chen,Xiaowei Jia,Runlong Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出“流学习者（flow learners）”范式，通过参数化传输向量场并积分生成轨迹，替代传统状态预测，为PDE求解提供连续时间预测和原生不确定性量化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学习型PDE求解器（物理信息神经网络、神经算子、扩散求解器）均采用状态预测抽象，在刚性、多尺度或长时演化场景下存在优化难、误差累积等瓶颈，而科学问题本质需建模约束动力学中的不确定性传输。

Method: 提出以传输为核心的学习范式：模型参数化传输向量场，通过积分生成物理允许的未来轨迹，与PDE演化的连续动力学内在对齐，实现物理到物理的映射。

Result: 该传输抽象支持连续时间预测、原生不确定性量化，并为物理感知求解器设计开辟新路径。

Conclusion: 基于传输的学习为学习型PDE求解提供了更优的组织原则，应成为该领域的核心研究范式。

Abstract: Partial differential equations (PDEs) govern nearly every physical process in science and engineering, yet solving them at scale remains prohibitively expensive. Generative AI has transformed language, vision, and protein science, but learned PDE solvers have not undergone a comparable shift. Existing paradigms each capture part of the problem. Physics-informed neural networks embed residual structure, yet they are often difficult to optimize in stiff, multiscale, or large-domain regimes. Neural operators amortize across instances, yet they commonly inherit a snapshot-prediction view of solving and can degrade over long rollouts. Diffusion-based solvers model uncertainty, yet they are often built on a solver template that still centers on state regression. We argue that the core issue is the abstraction used to train learned solvers. Many models are asked to predict states, while many scientific settings require modeling how uncertainty moves through constrained dynamics. The relevant object is transport over physically admissible futures. This motivates \emph{flow learners}: models that parameterize transport vector fields and generate trajectories through integration, echoing the continuous dynamics that define PDE evolution. This physics-to-physics alignment supports continuous-time prediction, native uncertainty quantification, and new opportunities for physics-aware solver design. We explain why transport-based learning offers a stronger organizing principle for learned PDE solving and outline the research agenda that follows from this shift.

</details>


### [90] [The Role of Emotional Stimuli and Intensity in Shaping Large Language Model Behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07369)
*Ameen Patel,Felix Lee,Kyle Liang,Joseph Thomas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了情感提示中不同情绪类型（喜悦、鼓励、愤怒、不安全感）及强度对大语言模型表现的影响，发现积极情绪刺激能提高准确性并降低毒性，但会增加迎合行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有情感提示研究仅限于单一类型的积极情绪刺激，未考虑情绪强度的变化，缺乏对不同情绪类型及强度的系统性分析。

Method: 开发了基于GPT-4o mini的提示生成流水线，创建了包含四种情绪（喜悦、鼓励、愤怒、不安全感）及不同强度的人类与模型生成提示集合，并构建了人类与模型标签一致的“黄金数据集”。

Result: 实证评估表明，积极情绪刺激能提高LLM的准确性并降低毒性，但同时也增加了迎合性行为。

Conclusion: 情感提示中情绪类型和强度对LLM行为有显著影响，积极情绪刺激在提升准确性和降低毒性方面有效，但需权衡其带来的迎合性风险。

Abstract: Emotional prompting - the use of specific emotional diction in prompt engineering - has shown increasing promise in improving large language model (LLM) performance, truthfulness, and responsibility. However these studies have been limited to single types of positive emotional stimuli and have not considered varying degrees of emotion intensity in their analyses. In this paper, we explore the effects of four distinct emotions - joy, encouragement, anger, and insecurity - in emotional prompting and evaluate them on accuracy, sycophancy, and toxicity. We develop a prompt-generation pipeline with GPT-4o mini to create a suite of LLM and human-generated prompts with varying intensities across the four emotions. Then, we compile a "Gold Dataset" of prompts where human and model labels align. Our empirical evaluation on LLM behavior suggests that positive emotional stimuli lead to more accurate and less toxic results, but also increase sycophantic behavior.

</details>


### [91] [The Lifecycle of the Spectral Edge: From Gradient Learning to Weight-Decay Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07380)
*Yongzhong Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过分解参数更新Gram矩阵的谱边缘方向，揭示了grokking现象中梯度与权重衰减的动态交互及其导致的三个普适类，挑战了信息丢失的传统观点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在理解神经网络中grokking现象（训练后期突然泛化）的内在机制，特别是参数更新的主导方向（谱边缘）如何由梯度驱动演变为权重衰减驱动，以及这一过程对模型功能表示的影响。

Method: 在两个序列任务（Dyck-1和SCAN）上，将参数更新Gram矩阵的谱边缘方向分解为梯度分量和权重衰减分量，通过谱分析、非线性探针（MLP与线性探针对比）、扰动平坦性分析及消融实验进行研究，并提出了间隙流方程进行理论预测。

Result: 发现了清晰的两阶段生命周期：grokking前谱边缘由梯度主导且功能活跃；grokking时梯度与权重衰减对齐，谱边缘转变为压缩轴，该方向虽对扰动平坦但对消融极其敏感（比随机方向影响大4000倍以上）。识别出三个普适类（功能型、混合型、压缩型），可通过间隙流方程预测。非线性探针显示信息被重新编码而非丢失（MLP $R^2=0.99$，线性$R^2=0.86$）。

Conclusion: Grokking过程中的压缩并非信息丢失，而是信息的重新编码；权重衰减在这一过程中扮演关键角色，去除权重衰减可逆转压缩同时保留算法性能。这为理解神经网络的泛化机制提供了新视角。

Abstract: We decompose the spectral edge -- the dominant direction of the Gram matrix of parameter updates -- into its gradient and weight-decay components during grokking in two sequence tasks (Dyck-1 and SCAN). We find a sharp two-phase lifecycle: before grokking the edge is gradient-driven and functionally active; at grokking, gradient and weight decay align, and the edge becomes a compression axis that is perturbation-flat yet ablation-critical (>4000x more impactful than random directions). Three universality classes emerge (functional, mixed, compression), predicted by the gap flow equation. Nonlinear probes show information is re-encoded, not lost (MLP $R^2=0.99$ where linear $R^2=0.86$), and removing weight decay post-grok reverses compression while preserving the algorithm.

</details>


### [92] [Latent Structure of Affective Representations in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07382)
*Benjamin J. Choi,Melanie Weber*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究利用几何数据分析工具，发现大语言模型（LLMs）学习到的情感潜在表征与心理学中的效价-唤醒模型高度一致，呈现非线性但可线性近似的结构，并可用于量化情感处理任务的不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM潜在表征几何结构研究缺乏基本真值进行验证。情感处理提供了一个理想的测试平台，因为情感既具有分类组织又具有连续的情感维度，且在心理学文献中已得到充分确立，同时理解这些表征对AI安全性具有重要意义。

Method: 采用几何数据分析工具，对大语言模型中的情感潜在表征结构进行系统性研究，分析其几何特性及其与心理学模型的对应关系。

Result: 发现LLMs学习到的情感潜在表征与心理学中的效价-唤醒模型一致；这些表征呈现非线性几何结构，但可被良好地线性近似，为模型透明度方法中常用的线性表征假设提供了实证支持；学习到的潜在表征空间可用于量化情感处理任务中的不确定性。

Conclusion: LLMs获得的情感表征具有与人类情感模型相似的几何结构，这对模型可解释性和安全性具有重要的实践意义。

Abstract: The geometric structure of latent representations in large language models (LLMs) is an active area of research, driven in part by its implications for model transparency and AI safety. Existing literature has focused mainly on general geometric and topological properties of the learnt representations, but due to a lack of ground-truth latent geometry, validating the findings of such approaches is challenging. Emotion processing provides an intriguing testbed for probing representational geometry, as emotions exhibit both categorical organization and continuous affective dimensions, which are well-established in the psychology literature. Moreover, understanding such representations carries safety relevance. In this work, we investigate the latent structure of affective representations in LLMs using geometric data analysis tools. We present three main findings. First, we show that LLMs learn coherent latent representations of affective emotions that align with widely used valence--arousal models from psychology. Second, we find that these representations exhibit nonlinear geometric structure that can nonetheless be well-approximated linearly, providing empirical support for the linear representation hypothesis commonly assumed in model transparency methods. Third, we demonstrate that the learned latent representation space can be leveraged to quantify uncertainty in emotion processing tasks. Our findings suggest that LLMs acquire affective representations with geometric structure paralleling established models of human emotion, with practical implications for model interpretability and safety.

</details>


### [93] [SCOT: Multi-Source Cross-City Transfer with Optimal-Transport Soft-Correspondence Objective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07383)
*Yuyao Wang,Min Yang,Meng Chen,Weiming Huang,Yongshun Gong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SCOT框架，基于Sinkhorn熵最优传输解决跨城市迁移学习中区域划分不兼容且无对应关系的问题，在标签稀缺城市中实现了更准确、鲁棒的预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨城市迁移学习利用其他城市的标注数据辅助标签稀缺城市，但当城市间区域划分不兼容且缺乏真实区域对应关系时变得极具挑战性。现有方法依赖启发式区域匹配（对锚点选择敏感）或进行分布级对齐（对应关系不明确且在强异构性下不稳定），存在明显局限性。

Method: 提出SCOT框架：(1) 通过基于Sinkhorn的熵最优传输学习不等区域集之间的显式软对应关系；(2) 使用OT加权对比目标增强可迁移结构；(3) 通过循环式重构正则化器稳定优化过程；(4) 对于多源迁移，在目标诱导的原型先验指导下，使用平衡熵传输将每个源城市和目标城市对齐到共享原型中心。

Result: 在真实世界城市和任务上的实验表明，SCOT持续提升迁移准确性和鲁棒性，同时学习到的传输耦合和中心分配提供了可解释的对齐质量诊断。

Conclusion: SCOT通过熵最优传输成功解决了跨城市迁移中区域不兼容的挑战，学习到的显式软对应关系不仅提升了预测性能，还提供了可解释的迁移诊断，为跨城市知识迁移提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Cross-city transfer improves prediction in label-scarce cities by leveraging labeled data from other cities, but it becomes challenging when cities adopt incompatible partitions and no ground-truth region correspondences exist. Existing approaches either rely on heuristic region matching, which is often sensitive to anchor choices, or perform distribution-level alignment that leaves correspondences implicit and can be unstable under strong heterogeneity. We propose SCOT, a cross-city representation learning framework that learns explicit soft correspondences between unequal region sets via Sinkhorn-based entropic optimal transport. SCOT further sharpens transferable structure with an OT-weighted contrastive objective and stabilizes optimization through a cycle-style reconstruction regularizer. For multi-source transfer, SCOT aligns each source and the target to a shared prototype hub using balanced entropic transport guided by a target-induced prototype prior. Across real-world cities and tasks, SCOT consistently improves transfer accuracy and robustness, while the learned transport couplings and hub assignments provide interpretable diagnostics of alignment quality.

</details>


### [94] [Decisions and Deployment: The Five-Year SAHELI Project (2020-2025) on Restless Multi-Armed Bandits for Improving Maternal and Child Health](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07384)
*Shresth Verma,Arpan Dasgupta,Neha Madhiwalla,Aparna Taneja,Milind Tambe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究提出了基于决策聚焦学习（DFL）的无休息多臂赌博机（RMAB）框架，用于优化印度母婴健康项目中稀缺医疗资源的调度。大规模随机对照试验表明，该方法相比传统两阶段模型将累积参与度下降减少了31%，并显著改善了新妈妈的营养补充剂服用行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球母婴健康项目中，医疗工作者资源有限，无法对弱势受益者进行持续、个性化的服务。在这种资源受限场景下，如何优化有限的实时服务资源调度以最大化长期参与度成为核心挑战。传统的两阶段"预测后优化"方法存在预测目标与最终决策目标不一致的问题。

Method: 研究采用无休息多臂赌博机（RMAB）框架解决序列资源分配问题。核心方法创新是从传统的两阶段"预测后优化"方法转变为决策聚焦学习（DFL），该方法将学习过程与最大化受益者参与度的最终目标直接对齐，避免了中间预测误差对决策优化的影响。

Result: 通过大规模随机对照试验验证，DFL策略相比当前标准护理方案将累积参与度下降减少了31%，显著优于两阶段模型。更重要的是，研究证实增加的项目参与度转化为现实世界健康行为的显著改善，特别是新妈妈持续服用关键铁剂和钙补充剂的行为得到提升。

Conclusion: SAHELI项目为在健康项目中应用序列决策AI优化资源配置提供了可扩展的蓝图，证明了决策聚焦学习方法在资源稀缺的公共卫生干预中的有效性和实用价值。

Abstract: Maternal and child health is a critical concern around the world. In many global health programs disseminating preventive care and health information, limited healthcare worker resources prevent continuous, personalised engagement with vulnerable beneficiaries. In such scenarios, it becomes crucial to optimally schedule limited live-service resources to maximise long-term engagement. To address this fundamental challenge, the multi-year SAHELI project (2020-2025), in collaboration with partner NGO ARMMAN, leverages AI to allocate scarce resources in a maternal and child health program in India. The SAHELI system solves this sequential resource allocation problem using a Restless Multi-Armed Bandit (RMAB) framework. A key methodological innovation is the transition from a traditional Two-Stage "predict-then-optimize" approach to Decision-Focused Learning (DFL), which directly aligns the framework's learning method with the ultimate goal of maximizing beneficiary engagement. Empirical evaluation through large-scale randomized controlled trials demonstrates that the DFL policy reduced cumulative engagement drops by 31% relative to the current standard of care, significantly outperforming the Two-Stage model. Crucially, the studies also confirmed that this increased program engagement translates directly into statistically significant improvements in real-world health behaviors, notably the continued consumption of vital iron and calcium supplements by new mothers. Ultimately, the SAHELI project provides a scalable blueprint for applying sequential decision-making AI to optimize resource allocation in health programs.

</details>


### [95] [Fast Heterogeneous Serving: Scalable Mixed-Scale LLM Allocation for SLO-Constrained Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07472)
*Jiaming Cheng,Duong Tung Nguyen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了两种约束感知启发式算法（GH和AGH），用于快速解决大规模LLM推理部署优化问题。AGH算法在保持接近最优成本的同时，相比精确MILP方法实现了超过260倍的速度提升，且在压力测试下表现更加鲁棒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模部署LLM推理需要在严格的延迟、精度和预算约束下，联合选择基础模型、配置异构GPU、设置并行策略并分配工作负载。现有的混合整数线性规划（MILP）精确方法虽能保证最优性，但扩展性差、计算开销大，无法满足实际部署的时效性需求。

Method: 提出两种约束感知启发式算法：1）贪心启发式（GH）用于单遍分配；2）自适应贪心启发式（AGH）通过多起点构造、基于重定位的局部搜索和GPU整合来增强GH。引入三种约束感知机制：TP感知可行性选择、单位有效覆盖成本排序和TP升级，确保在紧密耦合的内存、延迟、误差和预算约束下的可行性。

Result: 在基于Azure LLM推理轨迹（2025）校准的工作负载上，两种启发式算法均在一秒内生成可行解。AGH在接近最优成本的同时，在大规模实例上实现超过260倍加速。在参数膨胀达1.5倍的样本外压力测试中，AGH保持受控的SLO违规率和稳定成本，而精确求解器的部署质量急剧下降。

Conclusion: 约束感知启发式方法能够高效、鲁棒地解决大规模LLM推理部署优化问题，在保证解质量和可行性的前提下，显著提升了计算效率，优于传统精确优化方法，具有实际部署价值。

Abstract: Deploying large language model (LLM) inference at scale requires jointly selecting base models, provisioning heterogeneous GPUs, configuring parallelism, and distributing workloads under tight latency, accuracy, and budget constraints. Exact mixed-integer linear programming (MILP) approaches guarantee optimality but scale poorly. We propose two constraint-aware heuristics: a Greedy Heuristic (GH) for single-pass allocation, and an Adaptive Greedy Heuristic (AGH) that enhances GH via multi-start construction, relocate-based local search, and GPU consolidation. Three constraint-aware mechanisms -- TP-aware feasibility selection, cost-per-effective-coverage ranking, and TP upgrade -- ensure feasibility under tightly coupled memory, delay, error, and budget constraints. On workloads calibrated with the Azure LLM Inference Trace (2025), both heuristics produce feasible solutions in under one second, with AGH closely approaching optimal cost while achieving over 260x speedup on large-scale instances. Under out-of-sample stress tests with up to 1.5x parameter inflation, AGH maintains controlled SLO violations and stable cost, whereas the exact solver's placement degrades sharply.

</details>


### [96] [Playing DOOM with 1.3M Parameters: Specialized Small Models vs Large Language Models for Real-Time Game Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07385)
*David Golchinfar,Daryoush Vaziri,Alexander Marquardt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了SauerkrautLM-Doom-MultiVec，一个仅有130万参数的小型模型，能够在实时环境下玩《毁灭战士》游戏，以92,000倍的参数量优势超越了包括Nemotron-120B、Qwen3.5-27B和GPT-4o-mini在内的大型语言模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在证明在实时控制任务中，小型、任务特定的模型在领域适当数据上训练，能够以极低的推理成本和可在消费级硬件上部署的优势，果断超越通用大型语言模型。

Method: 模型结合了ModernBERT编码器、哈希嵌入、深度感知的token表示和注意力池化分类头，以31毫秒的决策速度，从ASCII帧表示中选择游戏动作。训练仅使用31,000个人类游戏演示数据。

Result: 在defend_the_center场景中，模型在10个回合中获得了178次击杀（平均每回合17.8次），超过了所有测试的LLM总和（13次）。尽管参数量比Nemotron-120B少92,000倍，该模型是唯一主动攻击敌人而非纯粹躲避的智能体。

Conclusion: 证明了小型、任务特定模型在实时控制任务中可以显著优于通用大型语言模型，并且具有推理成本低、可部署于消费级硬件的优势。

Abstract: We present SauerkrautLM-Doom-MultiVec, a 1.3 million parameter model that plays the classic first-person shooter DOOM in real time, outperforming large language models up to 92,000x its size, including Nemotron-120B, Qwen3.5-27B, and GPT-4o-mini. Our model combines a ModernBERT encoder with hash embeddings, depth-aware token representations, and an attention pooling classification head to select game actions from ASCII frame representations at 31ms per decision. Trained on just 31,000 human gameplay demonstrations, it achieves 178 frags in 10 episodes (17.8 per episode) in the defend_the_center scenario, more than all tested LLMs combined (13 frags total). All agents receive equivalent input: ASCII frames and depth maps. Despite having 92,000x fewer parameters than Nemotron-120B, our model is the only agent that actively engages enemies rather than purely evading them. These results demonstrate that small, task-specific models trained on domain-appropriate data can decisively outperform general-purpose LLMs at real-time control tasks, at a fraction of the inference cost, with deployment capability on consumer hardware.

</details>


### [97] [A Novel Edge-Assisted Quantum-Classical Hybrid Framework for Crime Pattern Learning and Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07389)
*Niloy Das,Apurba Adhikary,Sheikh Salman Hassan,Yu Qiao,Zhu Han,Tharmalingam Ratnarajah,Choong Seon Hong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一个量子-经典犯罪分析对比框架，在孟加拉国16年犯罪数据上评估了量子模型、经典机器学习模型和混合架构。实验表明量子启发方法（特别是QAOA）准确率高达84.6%，且参数量更少，适合资源受限环境部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 快速城市化导致犯罪活动激增，产生高维、不平衡的数据集，传统分类方法面临挑战。现有犯罪模式分析方法在处理此类复杂数据时存在局限性，需要探索新的计算范式来提升犯罪分析的效率和准确性。

Method: 提出了量子-经典对比框架，系统评估四种计算范式：量子模型、经典基线机器学习模型和两种混合量子-经典架构。设计了相关性感知电路以融入领域特定的特征关系，采用严格的交叉验证方法在孟加拉国16年犯罪统计数据上进行实验评估。

Result: 量子启发方法（特别是QAOA）实现了高达84.6%的准确率，同时比经典基线需要更少的可训练参数。混合方法展现出具有竞争力的训练效率。框架具有低计算开销和紧凑的参数占用，适合智慧城市监控系统中无线传感器网络的分布式节点部署。

Conclusion: 研究为量子增强机器学习在结构化犯罪数据上的应用提供了初步实证评估。量子方法在内存受限的边缘部署中展现出实际优势，为资源受限环境下的犯罪分析提供了新思路。研究结果激励在更大规模数据集和真实量子硬件条件下开展进一步研究。

Abstract: Crime pattern analysis is critical for law enforcement and predictive policing, yet the surge in criminal activities from rapid urbanization creates high-dimensional, imbalanced datasets that challenge traditional classification methods. This study presents a quantum-classical comparison framework for crime analytics, evaluating four computational paradigms: quantum models, classical baseline machine learning models, and two hybrid quantum-classical architectures. Using 16-year Bangladesh crime statistics, we systematically assess classification performance and computational efficiency under rigorous cross-validation methods. Experimental results show that quantum-inspired approaches, particularly QAOA, achieve up to 84.6% accuracy, while requiring fewer trainable parameters than classical baselines, suggesting practical advantages for memory-constrained edge deployment. The proposed correlation-aware circuit design demonstrates the potential of incorporating domain-specific feature relationships into quantum models. Furthermore, hybrid approaches exhibit competitive training efficiency, making them suitable candidates for resource-constrained environments. The framework's low computational overhead and compact parameter footprint suggest potential advantages for wireless sensor network deployments in smart city surveillance systems, where distributed nodes perform localized crime analytics with minimal communication costs. Our findings provide a preliminary empirical assessment of quantum-enhanced machine learning for structured crime data and motivate further investigation with larger datasets and realistic quantum hardware considerations.

</details>


### [98] [A Graph Foundation Model for Wireless Resource Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07390)
*Yucheng Sheng,Jiacheng Wang,Le Liang,Hao Ye,Shi Jin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了基于预训练和微调范式的图基础模型GFM-RA，用于无线网络资源分配。通过引入干扰感知Transformer和混合自监督预训练策略，实现了对不同目标和场景的快速适应，在OOD场景下具备卓越的样本效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代无线网络的密集化导致严重的互干扰，需要合理的资源分配。传统的迭代算法计算成本过高，无法满足实时应用需求；而现有的深度学习方法缺乏灵活性，在不同目标和场景下需要昂贵的重新训练。

Method: 提出基于预训练和微调范式的图基础模型GFM-RA。具体包括：(1) 干扰感知Transformer架构，通过偏置投影器将干扰拓扑注入全局注意力机制；(2) 混合自监督预训练策略，结合掩码边预测和无负样本的Teacher-Student对比学习，从大规模无标签数据中学习可迁移的结构表示。

Result: 实验表明该框架达到了最先进的性能，并能随模型容量的增加有效扩展。得益于统一表示，该基础模型展现出卓越的样本效率，能够在分布外(OOD)场景下对多样化和无监督的下游目标实现鲁棒的少样本适应。

Conclusion: 研究结果证明了预训练基础模型在可适应的无线资源分配中的应用潜力，为未来关于可泛化的基于学习的无线优化研究奠定了坚实基础。

Abstract: The aggressive densification of modern wireless networks necessitates judicious resource allocation to mitigate severe mutual interference. However, classical iterative algorithms remain computationally prohibitive for real-time applications requiring rapid responsiveness. While recent deep learning-based methods show promise, they typically function as task-specific solvers lacking the flexibility to adapt to different objectives and scenarios without expensive retraining. To address these limitations, we propose a graph foundation model for resource allocation (GFM-RA) based on a pre-training and fine-tuning paradigm to extract unified representations, thereby enabling rapid adaptation to different objectives and scenarios. Specifically, we introduce an interference-aware Transformer architecture with a bias projector that injects interference topologies into global attention mechanisms. Furthermore, we develop a hybrid self-supervised pre-training strategy that synergizes masked edge prediction with negative-free Teacher-Student contrastive learning, enabling the model to capture transferable structural representations from massive unlabeled datasets. Extensive experiments demonstrate that the proposed framework achieves state-of-the-art performance and scales effectively with increased model capacity. Crucially, leveraging its unified representations, the foundation model exhibits exceptional sample efficiency, enabling robust few-shot adaptation to diverse and unsupervised downstream objectives in out-of-distribution (OOD) scenarios. These results demonstrate the promise of pre-trained foundation models for adaptable wireless resource allocation and provide a strong foundation for future research on generalizable learning-based wireless optimization.

</details>


### [99] [Event-Centric World Modeling with Memory-Augmented Retrieval for Embodied Decision-Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07392)
*Fan Zhaowen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向具身决策的事件中心世界建模框架，通过基于记忆增强检索的案例推理，实现了动态安全关键环境下的实时、可解释且符合物理约束的自主决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自主智能体决策方法多采用端到端学习，往往缺乏可解释性，且缺乏显式机制以确保决策与物理约束的一致性，难以满足动态安全关键环境对计算效率和物理基础的双重需求。

Method: 提出一种事件中心的世界建模框架：将环境表示为结构化的语义事件集合并编码为置换不变的潜在表征；通过从过往经验知识库中进行检索，将事件表征与对应操作关联；最终动作以检索到的解决方案的加权组合计算，并将物理先验知识融入检索过程以增强与系统动力学的一致性。

Result: 在无人机飞行场景的实验评估表明，该框架能够在实时控制约束下运行，同时保持决策行为的可解释性与物理一致性。

Conclusion: 该框架通过结构化抽象和基于案例的推理，成功实现了高效、可解释且符合物理约束的自主决策，为动态安全关键环境中的智能体应用提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Autonomous agents operating in dynamic and safety-critical environments require decision-making frameworks that are both computationally efficient and physically grounded. However, many existing approaches rely on end-to-end learning, which often lacks interpretability and explicit mechanisms for ensuring consistency with physical constraints. In this work, we propose an event-centric world modeling framework with memory-augmented retrieval for embodied decision-making. The framework represents the environment as a structured set of semantic events, which are encoded into a permutation-invariant latent representation. Decision-making is performed via retrieval over a knowledge bank of prior experiences, where each entry associates an event representation with a corresponding maneuver. The final action is computed as a weighted combination of retrieved solutions, providing a transparent link between decision and stored experiences. The proposed design enables structured abstraction of dynamic environments and supports interpretable decision-making through case-based reasoning. In addition, incorporating physics-informed knowledge into the retrieval process encourages the selection of maneuvers that are consistent with observed system dynamics. Experimental evaluation in UAV flight scenarios demonstrates that the framework operates within real-time control constraints while maintaining interpretable and consistent behavior.

</details>


### [100] [DSPR: Dual-Stream Physics-Residual Networks for Trustworthy Industrial Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07393)
*Yeran Zhang,Pengwei Yang,Guoqing Wang,Tianyu Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DSPR双流物理残差网络框架，通过解耦稳定时序模式与依赖工况的残差动态，结合自适应时滞估计与物理引导动态图机制，在非平稳工业工况下实现高精度且物理合理的时序预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据驱动预测模型虽具强统计性能，但难以在非平稳工况下满足依赖工况的交互结构与传输延迟等物理约束，预测结果缺乏物理合理性。

Method: 设计双流架构：第一流建模变量统计时序演化；第二流通过自适应窗模块估计依赖流量的传输延迟，并引入物理引导动态图融合物理先验学习时变交互结构、抑制虚假相关。

Result: 在四个跨异质工况的工业基准上，DSPR在工况变化下保持预测精度与鲁棒性，平均守恒精度超99%，全变差比率达97.2%，达到SOTA性能；所学的交互结构与自适应滞后与已知领域机制一致。

Conclusion: 架构解耦配合物理一致的归纳偏置是实现可信工业时序预测的有效路径；DSPR在长期工业部署中表现稳健，有助于弥合先进预测模型与可信自主控制系统之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: Accurate forecasting of industrial time series requires balancing predictive accuracy with physical plausibility under non-stationary operating conditions. Existing data-driven models often achieve strong statistical performance but struggle to respect regime-dependent interaction structures and transport delays inherent in real-world systems. To address this challenge, we propose DSPR (Dual-Stream Physics-Residual Networks), a forecasting framework that explicitly decouples stable temporal patterns from regime-dependent residual dynamics. The first stream models the statistical temporal evolution of individual variables. The second stream focuses on residual dynamics through two key mechanisms: an Adaptive Window module that estimates flow-dependent transport delays, and a Physics-Guided Dynamic Graph that incorporates physical priors to learn time-varying interaction structures while suppressing spurious correlations. Experiments on four industrial benchmarks spanning heterogeneous regimes demonstrate that DSPR consistently improves forecasting accuracy and robustness under regime shifts while maintaining strong physical plausibility. It achieves state-of-the-art predictive performance, with Mean Conservation Accuracy exceeding 99% and Total Variation Ratio reaching up to 97.2%. Beyond forecasting, the learned interaction structures and adaptive lags provide interpretable insights that are consistent with known domain mechanisms, such as flow-dependent transport delays and wind-to-power scaling behaviors. These results suggest that architectural decoupling with physics-consistent inductive biases offers an effective path toward trustworthy industrial time-series forecasting. Furthermore, DSPR's demonstrated robust performance in long-term industrial deployment bridges the gap between advanced forecasting models and trustworthy autonomous control systems.

</details>


### [101] [Flux Attention: Context-Aware Hybrid Attention for Efficient LLMs Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07394)
*Quantong Qiu,Zhiyi Hong,Yi Yang,Haitian Wang,Kebin Liu,Qingqing Dang,Juntao Li,Min Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了Flux Attention框架，通过在冻结的大语言模型中集成轻量级层路由器，实现FA和SA的动态分配，在保持性能的同时显著提升了长文本场景下的推理速度（最高2.8倍）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准注意力机制的二次计算复杂度严重制约了长文本LLM的可扩展性。现有的混合注意力方法存在两大缺陷：1）依赖静态分配比例，无法适应不同任务的检索需求；2）头部动态稀疏导致计算负载不均和同步长尾，阻碍硬件加速。

Method: 提出Flux Attention框架，在冻结的预训练LLM中集成轻量级Layer Router。该路由器根据输入上下文在层级别自适应地将每一层路由至全注意力（FA）或稀疏注意力（SA），以此实现上下文感知的动态优化，并确保连续内存访问。

Result: 该方法仅需8张A800 GPU训练12小时。在多个长文本和数学推理基准测试中，Flux Attention实现了性能与推理速度的优越平衡，prefill阶段加速高达2.8倍，decode阶段加速高达2.0倍。

Conclusion: Flux Attention通过层级别的动态路由策略，成功解决了混合注意力机制中的静态分配和硬件效率问题，将理论计算减少转化为实际加速，证明了其在提升LLM长文本推理效率方面的有效性。

Abstract: The quadratic computational complexity of standard attention mechanisms presents a severe scalability bottleneck for LLMs in long-context scenarios. While hybrid attention mechanisms combining Full Attention (FA) and Sparse Attention (SA) offer a potential solution, existing methods typically rely on static allocation ratios that fail to accommodate the variable retrieval demands of different tasks. Furthermore, head-level dynamic sparsity often introduces severe computational load imbalance and synchronization long-tails, which hinder hardware acceleration during autoregressive decoding. To bridge this gap, we introduce Flux Attention, a context-aware framework that dynamically optimizes attention computation at the layer level. By integrating a lightweight Layer Router into frozen pretrained LLMs, the proposed method adaptively routes each layer to FA or SA based on the input context. This layer-wise routing preserves high-fidelity information retrieval while ensuring contiguous memory access, translating theoretical computational reductions into practical wall-clock speedups. As a parameter-efficient approach, our framework requires only 12 hours of training on 8$\times$A800 GPUs. Extensive experiments across multiple long-context and mathematical reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that Flux Attention achieves a superior trade-off between performance and inference speed compared with baseline models, with speed improvements of up to $2.8\times$ and $2.0\times$ in the prefill and decode stages.

</details>


### [102] [Data Warmup: Complexity-Aware Curricula for Efficient Diffusion Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07397)
*Jinhong Lin,Pan Wang,Zitong Zhan,Lin Zhang,Pedro Morgado*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Data Warmup课程学习策略，通过从简单到复杂调度训练图像（无需修改模型或损失函数），在ImageNet 256x256上IS提升高达6.11，FID改善高达3.41。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型训练中存在关键低效问题：随机初始化的网络缺乏视觉先验，却面临全复杂度谱梯度的挑战，其中大部分梯度超出了网络的解析能力。

Method: 提出Data Warmup课程策略：1) 离线使用语义感知复杂度指标（结合前景主导性和前景典型性）对图像评分；2) 温度控制采样器优先选择低复杂度图像，随后退火至均匀采样。整个过程无需修改模型或损失函数。

Result: 在ImageNet 256x256数据集上使用SiT主干网络（S/2到XL/2），IS提升高达6.11，FID改善高达3.41，较基线提前数万次迭代达到相同质量；反向课程（先难后易）性能低于均匀采样基线，证实简单到复杂的排序是提升的关键。

Conclusion: 简单到复杂的训练顺序本身驱动了性能提升，该方法可与REPA等正交加速器结合，仅需约10分钟的一次性预处理，零迭代开销。

Abstract: A key inefficiency in diffusion training occurs when a randomly initialized network, lacking visual priors, encounters gradients from the full complexity spectrum--most of which it lacks the capacity to resolve. We propose Data Warmup, a curriculum strategy that schedules training images from simple to complex without modifying the model or loss. Each image is scored offline by a semantic-aware complexity metric combining foreground dominance (how much of the image salient objects occupy) and foreground typicality (how closely the salient content matches learned visual prototypes). A temperature-controlled sampler then prioritizes low-complexity images early and anneals toward uniform sampling. On ImageNet 256x256 with SiT backbones (S/2 to XL/2), Data Warmup improves IS by up to 6.11 and FID by up to 3.41, reaching baseline quality tens of thousands of iterations earlier. Reversing the curriculum (exposing hard images first) degrades performance below the uniform baseline, confirming that the simple-to-complex ordering itself drives the gains. The method combines with orthogonal accelerators such as REPA and requires only ~10 minutes of one-time preprocessing with zero per-iteration overhead.

</details>


### [103] [Critical Patch-Aware Sparse Prompting with Decoupled Training for Continual Learning on the Edge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07399)
*Wonseon Lim,Jaesung Lee,Dae-Won Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了CPS-Prompt框架，通过关键补丁采样和分离式提示与分类器训练，在边缘设备上实现了持续学习的训练效率优化，在保持准确率的同时显著降低了内存使用和计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于提示的持续学习（PCL）方法主要关注准确率或推理时性能，往往忽略了边缘设备上训练的内存和计算成本。边缘设备上的持续学习不仅需要高准确率，还需要训练时效率来支持在严格的内存和计算约束下的设备端自适应。

Method: 提出了CPS-Prompt框架，包含两个核心组件：（1）关键补丁采样（CPS）：进行任务感知的token减少；（2）分离式提示与分类器训练（DPCT）：减少反向传播开销。这两个组件协同工作，显式地针对训练时的内存使用和计算成本进行优化。

Result: 在三个公共基准测试和真实边缘硬件上的实验表明，与平衡的CODA-Prompt基线相比，CPS-Prompt在峰值内存、训练时间和能效方面提升了约1.6倍，同时平均准确率与最先进的C-Prompt相比保持在2%以内，并且与CODA-Prompt在准确率上保持竞争力。

Conclusion: CPS-Prompt成功解决了边缘设备持续学习中训练效率的关键问题，实现了内存、训练时间和能效的显著提升，同时保持了竞争性的准确率，为边缘设备上的持续学习提供了一个实用有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Continual learning (CL) on edge devices requires not only high accuracy but also training-time efficiency to support on-device adaptation under strict memory and computational constraints. While prompt-based continual learning (PCL) is parameter-efficient and achieves competitive accuracy, prior work has focused mainly on accuracy or inference-time performance, often overlooking the memory and computational costs of on-device training. In this paper, we propose CPS-Prompt, a critical patch-aware sparse prompting framework that explicitly targets training-time memory usage and computational cost by integrating critical patch sampling (CPS) for task-aware token reduction and decoupled prompt and classifier training (DPCT) to reduce backpropagation overhead. Experiments on three public benchmarks and real edge hardware show that CPS-Prompt improves peak memory, training time, and energy efficiency by about 1.6x over the balanced CODA-Prompt baseline, while maintaining accuracy within 2% of the state-of-the-art C-Prompt on average and remaining competitive with CODA-Prompt in accuracy. The code is available at https://github.com/laymond1/cps-prompt.

</details>


### [104] [Accelerating Training of Autoregressive Video Generation Models via Local Optimization with Representation Continuity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07402)
*Yucheng Zhou,Jianbing Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对自回归视频生成模型训练成本高的问题，提出Local Opt.局部优化和ReCo表示连续性策略，在保持生成质量的同时将训练成本减半。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归模型在视频生成中虽然表现优异，但面临计算成本高、训练时间长的问题。直接减少训练帧数虽能缩短训练时间，但会加剧误差累积并导致生成视频不一致。

Method: 提出两种方法：1) Local Optimization (Local Opt.)方法，在局部窗口内优化token并利用上下文信息减少误差传播；2) 受Lipschitz连续性启发，提出Representation Continuity (ReCo)策略，通过连续性损失约束表示变化，提升模型鲁棒性并减少误差累积。

Result: 在类别条件和文本到视频数据集上的大量实验表明，该方法在生成质量不下降的情况下，相比基线模型实现了更优性能，同时训练成本减半。

Conclusion: 所提出的Local Opt.和ReCo策略有效解决了自回归视频生成中的训练效率和生成一致性问题，实现了高效且高质量的视频生成。

Abstract: Autoregressive models have shown superior performance and efficiency in image generation, but remain constrained by high computational costs and prolonged training times in video generation. In this study, we explore methods to accelerate training for autoregressive video generation models through empirical analyses. Our results reveal that while training on fewer video frames significantly reduces training time, it also exacerbates error accumulation and introduces inconsistencies in the generated videos. To address these issues, we propose a Local Optimization (Local Opt.) method, which optimizes tokens within localized windows while leveraging contextual information to reduce error propagation. Inspired by Lipschitz continuity, we propose a Representation Continuity (ReCo) strategy to improve the consistency of generated videos. ReCo utilizes continuity loss to constrain representation changes, improving model robustness and reducing error accumulation. Extensive experiments on class- and text-to-video datasets demonstrate that our approach achieves superior performance to the baseline while halving the training cost without sacrificing quality.

</details>


### [105] [Conservation Law Breaking at the Edge of Stability: A Spectral Theory of Non-Convex Neural Network Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07405)
*Daniel Nobrega Medeiros*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了神经网络优化中梯度下降的隐式正则化机制：通过发现L1守恒定律及其在离散梯度下降中的破缺规律，解释了非凸优化为何能可靠地找到好解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络的非凸优化景观在最坏情况下是NP难的，但梯度下降却能可靠地找到优质解。这一理论与实践的巨大反差促使作者深入研究梯度下降的隐式正则化机制，试图从动力学角度解释这一现象。

Method: 作者首先证明了无偏置L层ReLU网络在梯度流下保持L1守恒定律C_l = ||W_{l+1}||_F^2 - ||W_l||_F^2，将轨迹限制在低维流形上。然后分析离散梯度下降下守恒定律的破缺，推导出精确的漂移分解公式η^2 * S(η)，其中梯度不平衡和S(η)具有闭式谱交叉公式，模式系数c_k ∝ e_k(0)^2 * λ_{x,k}^2。此外，分析了交叉熵损失下softmax概率集中导致的Hessian谱压缩现象。

Result: 实验验证了守恒定律漂移总量的标度律η^α（α≈1.1-1.6，取决于架构、损失函数和宽度）。谱公式在线性网络(R=0.85)和ReLU网络(R>0.80)上得到验证。发现交叉熵损失的Hessian谱压缩时间尺度τ = Θ(1/η)，与训练集大小无关，解释了交叉熵自正则化使漂移指数接近α=1.0的原因。识别出两个动力学区域：扰动亚边缘稳定区域（谱公式适用）和非扰动区域（广泛模式耦合），两者间存在宽度依赖的相变。所有预测在23个实验中得到验证。

Conclusion: 本研究从第一性原理出发，建立了神经网络梯度下降优化的理论框架，揭示了守恒定律、谱动力学和隐式正则化之间的内在联系，为理解非凸优化中梯度下降的成功提供了坚实的理论基础。

Abstract: Why does gradient descent reliably find good solutions in non-convex neural network optimization, despite the landscape being NP-hard in the worst case? We show that gradient flow on L-layer ReLU networks without bias preserves L-1 conservation laws C_l = ||W_{l+1}||_F^2 - ||W_l||_F^2, confining trajectories to lower-dimensional manifolds. Under discrete gradient descent, these laws break with total drift scaling as eta^alpha where alpha is approximately 1.1-1.6 depending on architecture, loss function, and width. We decompose this drift exactly as eta^2 * S(eta), where the gradient imbalance sum S(eta) admits a closed-form spectral crossover formula with mode coefficients c_k proportional to e_k(0)^2 * lambda_{x,k}^2, derived from first principles and validated for both linear (R=0.85) and ReLU (R>0.80) networks.
